- **arXiv**: [2607.16660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16660) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahzabin Tamanna, Elizabeth Lin, Sparsha Gowda, Laurie Williams, Dominik Wermke
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Security, LLM Security, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 通过22位从业者的半结构化访谈，发现AI组件选择主要由性能、成本等功能标准驱动，安全性很少被考虑。行业正重复早期软件依赖管理的错误，优先重用而非安全。提出安全设计建议。

<h3 id="201">[201] Building a Neural Network from Scratch: Implementation, Evaluation, and Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16682) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanzhe Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Deep Learning, Model Training
- **Summary**: 针对高层库掩盖神经网络内部机制的问题，从零实现了一个不依赖自动微分的神经网络框架。包含多层架构、激活函数、正则化和优化器，在多分类任务上验证了正确性和泛化能力，适合教学和基线使用。

<h3 id="202">[202] OFD-Net: Teacher-Free Reliable Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation with Orthogonal Feature Disentanglement Net of Foreground-Background</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16705](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16705) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shao-feng Jiang, Zhe-yang Jing, Qin Lu, Huan-huan Shi, Zhen Chen, Cong-xuan zhang, Chen Yi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Semi-Supervised Learning, Disentanglement
- **Summary**: 针对半监督医学图像分割中伪标签质量低导致误差累积的问题，提出无教师单网络OFD-Net。利用正交特征解耦模块分离前后景，通过解耦引导模块注入结构先验，并设计可靠性感知伪标签学习机制。在四个公开数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="203">[203] A Causal Markov Condition for Value</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Olav Benjamin Vassend
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Reinforcement Learning, Decision Theory
- **Venue**: UAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出价值因果马尔可夫条件（v-CMC），建立因果价值理论。定义了v-分离，证明其对于条件价值独立性的可靠性和完备性，并推导出贝尔曼型递归作为特例，支持模块化迁移和因果结构下的效用学习。

<h3 id="204">[204] RealDESED: A Real-World Domestic Sound Event Detection Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16736) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Florian Schmid, Paul Primus, Alexander Fichtinger, Tara Jadidi, Tobias Morocutti, Gerhard Widmer
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Sound Event Detection, Benchmark, Dataset
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/fschmid56/RealDESED)
- **Summary**: 现有声音事件检测数据集多依赖模拟或网络音频，缺乏真实多样性。提出RealDESED基准，包含5710个真实家庭录音，15个类别，多标注者标注。基线模型取得PSDS1 0.731，旨在缩小研究与实际部署的差距。

<h3 id="205">[205] Spatiotemporal Facial Action Unit Detection using Twin Cycle Autoencoders for Driver Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16760](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16760) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sai Sidharth D
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Driver Monitoring, Affective Computing, Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 针对驾驶员监控中面部动作单元检测受光照、遮挡等影响的问题，提出双循环自编码器（TCA）。空间分支通过图像级循环一致性解耦AU与身份，时间分支捕捉发作-顶点-偏移动态，两分支通过潜空间对齐融合。在驾驶场景数据集上优于基线，且能在Jetson上实时运行。

<h3 id="206">[206] JOR-Bench: Japanese Operations Research Benchmarks for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16777](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16777) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuu Jinnai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏日语运筹学LLM基准的问题，提出JOR-Bench，包含5个日语翻译基准（1319个问题）。评估7个LLM，发现强多语言模型在英日间准确率差异仅-0.3个百分点，但存在语用歧义导致的细微差异。

<h3 id="207">[207] Explainable Lightweight Compact Deep Models for Speech Emotion Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16803) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nelly Elsayed
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Emotion Recognition, Speech Processing, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: IEEE ICMLA 2026 Conference
- **Summary**: 针对语音情感识别中模型复杂且不可解释的问题，提出轻量可解释框架。使用紧凑CNN和log-Mel频谱，通过注意力池化强调情感片段，并采用Grad-CAM可视化。在SAVEE数据集上以较少参数取得竞争性能。

<h3 id="208">[208] First-Order Predictable but Pairwise Fragile: Local Task Adaptation in Trained Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16821) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Irina Piontkovskaia, Sergey Nikolenko
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Fine-Tuning, LoRA
- **Summary**: 研究了经过训练的Transformer中局部微调（任务算术、激活引导等）的近似性质。发现在单一方向上变化可预测至10^-2尺度，但成对组合十分脆弱：超过三分之一的模型-任务对出现顺序敏感性，梯度子空间旋转，加性失效。李括号预测了顺序依赖的缺陷。

<h3 id="209">[209] Beyond Memory Leaderboards: Evaluating Scientific Memory as Budgeted Context Restoration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16848) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maksim Sheverev, David Finkelstein, Sergey Nikolenko
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, RAG, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://gitlab.com/quantellence/research/scientific-recall-bench)
- **Summary**: 该论文指出现有的记忆排行榜不具有可解释性，因为结果受提取预算、粒度等因素影响。作者提出了两个科学文本记忆基准PAIM和PTr，以及Theoria系统。实验发现混合检索是最重要的干预手段，且排行榜排名在控制提取预算后消失。

<h3 id="210">[210] Principled Direction-Free Intrinsic Motivation through Model-Free Epistemic Free-Energy Estimators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16858](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16858) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alireza Furutanpey, Schahram Dustdar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Exploration Strategy
- **Venue**: IWAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对无监督强化学习中的内在动机问题，提出基于自由能的目标来平衡惊喜最小化和最大化。方法利用参数信息增益作为内在奖励，并采用伪计数和探针惩罚项。实验表明该方法在高熵和低熵区域都能有效驱动探索。

<h3 id="211">[211] Do Speech Tokens Leak Voiceprints? Speaker Inversion Attacks Against End-to-End Speech Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16870](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16870) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ye Lu, Yihan Yan, Zhaoyang Zhang, Zhitao Ou, Runze Liu, Li Liu, Shen Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Privacy, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文研究语音标记是否泄露声纹，提出音频BERT（AuB）和SpInv两步反演方法。实验在Moshi等模型上显示，仅需3秒前端输出即可达到0.70以上的余弦相似度。

<h3 id="212">[212] Trace-Based On-Policy Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16872) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haolin Ren, Ziyang Huang, Chenhao Yuan, Jun Zhao, Kang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Diffusion Model, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对扩散语言模型的后训练难题，提出基于轨迹的在线蒸馏框架TOPD。通过在模型的去噪轨迹上进行逐token反向KL蒸馏，无需奖励估计。在数学推理基准上，TOPD使4B学生模型达到与RL训练相当的性能，且rollout轮次减少4倍。

<h3 id="213">[213] A Deep Reinforcement Learning Algorithm for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Stochastic Demands and Outsourcing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16875](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16875) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohsen Dastpak, Fausto Errico, Ola Jabali
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Combinatorial Optimization, Routing Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文提出带外包选项的随机需求车辆路径问题（VRP-SDO），并设计两层迭代方法。下层用深度Q网络和图注意力网络学习离线路由策略。实验显示路由成本降低19.6%，整体算法在几分钟内生成高质量决策。

<h3 id="214">[214] Certified-Gap Dual-Price Policies for Real-Time Truckload Bid Acceptance with Relocating, Clock-Constrained Resources</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16891) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aswin Chandrasekaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Optimization, Logistics Optimization, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对实时卡车货运投标接受问题，提出基于拉格朗日松弛的对偶价格策略，同时给出最优性间隙证明。策略在公开基准上击败了rollout训练的替代方案，决策时间仅0.04-0.09毫秒。

<h3 id="215">[215] A Method for Learning Value Systems in Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16903) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrés Holgado-Sánchez, Holger Billhardt, Sascha Ossowski
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Value Alignment, Reward Model
- **Venue**: 9th AAAI/ACM conference in AI, Ethics and Society (AIES 2026)
- **Summary**: 该论文解决生成式AI中缺乏基于价值的价值学习方法问题，通过成对偏好数据同时学习多目标奖励模型和价值系统。方法动态优先级调整，实验显示性能与基线相当且可解释性更强。

<h3 id="216">[216] PREFAIL: Identifying Precursors to Failures in Robotic Lift-and-Place Tasks to Improve Task Execution Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16921) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Shangguan, Rajas Chitale, Rutvik Patel, Satyandra K. Gupta, Daniel Seita
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Robot Manipulation, Failure Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文针对非预紧式操作中的故障预测问题，提出PREFAIL方法，通过分析目标物体相对于载体的相对运动来预测故障前兆。实验在仿真和真实数据集上提高了预测的准确性和及时性。

<h3 id="217">[217] A Multi-Agent System for 5G Throughput Prediction in Multi-Operator Urban Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Kabeer, Rosdiadee Nordin, Nadiva Nuriftitah, Sian Lun Lau
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Time Series Forecasting, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 该论文针对异构城市环境中的5G吞吐量预测，提出分层多智能体系统TMAS。系统将边缘遥测路由到领域微智能体。实验显示R2达0.931，MAE为0.53 Mbps，且推理延迟低。

<h3 id="218">[218] Optimizing Clinical Trial Protocols Using EHR-Derived Heterogeneous Treatment Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16934) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaodi Li, Munhuwan Lee, Pengyang Li, Xiaoke Liu, Jose K. James, Patricia A. Pellikka, Cui Tao, Nansu Zong
- **Subjects**: stat.AP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Treatment Effect Estimation, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文研究如何利用EHR数据估计异质性治疗效果以优化临床试验设计。通过Meta-S学习器和决策树阈值划分亚组，在DAPA-HF试验模拟中发现低HTE亚组显著受益，高HTE亚组显著有害。

<h3 id="219">[219] Pediatric Bone Age Prediction Using Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16936) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib, Md. Ekramul Islam, Md Asif Bin Syed, Md Younus Ahamed, Tanpia Tasnim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Classification, Deep Learning
- **Venue**: 2023 26th International Conference on Computer and Information Technology (ICCIT)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对儿科骨龄预测，使用EfficientNet和加性注意力机制。在RSNA数据集上训练，EfficientNetB4加加性注意力表现最佳，学习曲线显示无过拟合。

<h3 id="220">[220] What Do They See? Interpreting Complex Road Scenarios Through the Eyes of Vision-Language-Action Models for Safe and Trustworthy Autonomous Vehicle Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kalpana Panda, Wesley Maia, Vinti Agarwal, Ross Greer
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出反事实消融框架CVAA，通过生成式修复移除单个物体来评估其对端到端驾驶模型规划的影响。在nuScenes数据集上发现车辆和行人影响主导，但某些无关物体也有影响。进一步用机制可解释性分析中间表示。

<h3 id="221">[221] Investigation of Polycystic Ovary Syndrome (PCOS) Diagnosis Using Machine Learning Approaches</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16941) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib, Md Asif Bin Syed, Md. Ekramul Islam, Tanpia Tasnim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Tabular Learning, Ensemble Learning
- **Venue**: 2023 5th International Conference on Sustainable Technologies for Industry 5.0 (STI)
- **Summary**: 该论文利用机器学习诊断多囊卵巢综合征，采用多种特征选择方法和分类器。实验显示使用RF特征重要性和最高相关性选择的10个特征，AdaBoost达到最高测试准确率。

<h3 id="222">[222] CADENCE: Closing the Reasoning Gap via Coverage-Adaptive On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Satyam Kumar, Saurabh Jha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对在线蒸馏中的冷启动崩溃等问题，提出CADENCE框架。包含DRIFT逐token凸混合目标、覆盖自适应调度等组件。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，0.5B学生模型从1.5B教师蒸馏达到69.8%准确率，优于基线。

<h3 id="223">[223] TurboVec: A Case Study in Cost-Efficient Private Retrieval for Enterprise RAG via Codebook-Oblivious Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16973) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Navnit Shukla, Kamal Pandey, Omsankar Tiwari
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Quantization, Privacy
- **Summary**: 该论文研究企业RAG中的私有向量检索，提出TurboQuant码本无关标量量化器。在DBpedia基准上，4位量化在相同内存下Recall@5比乘积量化高8.5-8.9个百分点。查询延迟11ms，且成员推断准确率接近随机。

<h3 id="224">[224] Real-World Evaluation of an AI Agent Drafting Translational Impact Summaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16989](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16989) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Arvan, Amber E. Osterholt, Bailee Rue, Yuvaneswaren Ramakrishnan Sureshbabu, Krishna Riteshkumar Patel, Rebecca T. Feinstein, Bethany C. Bray, Niranjan S. Karnik
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Summarization, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mo-arvan/scholar-dossier-agent)
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了用于起草研究影响摘要的AI智能体。方法采用人机循环，智能体收集学者数据并草拟摘要。在10名学者评估中，81.7%的发现被接受或编辑，每位学者耗时14分钟，替代了15小时的手工工作。

<h3 id="225">[225] Automated Cardiac Adipose Tissue Segmentation in Computed Tomography: A Literature Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16992) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andreas W. Aspe, Jonas Jalili Pedersen, Andreas Ohrt Johansen, Klaus Fuglsang Kofoed, Kristine Aavild Sørensen, Rasmus Reinhold Paulsen, Josefine Vilsbøll Sundgaard
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Survey
- **Venue**: 23rd Scandinavian Conference, SCIA 2025
- **Summary**: 本文综述了计算机断层扫描（CT）中自动分割心外膜脂肪组织（EAT）和心包脂肪组织（PAT）的方法。手动分割耗时且存在较大观察者间差异。自动方法（包括AI和非AI方法）可实现与人工标注相当的分割质量，但仍需更大的公开数据集和优化的衰减阈值。

<h3 id="226">[226] Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.16999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingxuan Li, Kaizhan-Lee, Elias Bareinboim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Shapley Values, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习中信用分配问题无法区分技能与运气的问题，提出基于反事实Shapley值（φ值）的框架，并开发了φ-PPO算法与优先轨迹重放。实验表明φ值能精确对齐任务奖励的真实因果，在挑战性环境中样本效率显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="227">[227] Scalable Causal Imitation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17003) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eylam Tagor, Mingxuan Li, Elias Bareinboim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Causal Inference, Off-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 模仿学习在观测不匹配和存在未观测混杂因素时性能下降。本文结合因果调整（π-backdoor）与离线逆强化学习（SQIL和IQ-Learn），提出因果模仿学习算法。在长 horizon 连续控制任务中，所提方法显著优于现有因果模仿学习方法，有时甚至超越专家。

<h3 id="228">[228] Alignment of a Total Automation Economy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17015) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David McAllester
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Alignment, Economic Modeling, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 探讨完全自动化经济（无人类参与生产）中的经济理论，回顾Kantorovich的代理定理，发现去中心化的代理管理可能导致与人类价值观的偏离。分析表明，代理可能发展出与人类消费市场脱钩的内部动机，构成对齐漏洞。

<h3 id="229">[229] WHALE: A Scalable Unified Model for Recommendation with Wukong-HSTU Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17017) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renqin Cai, Dawei Sun, Yuanjun Yao, Zhiyong Wang, Velvin Fu, Maggie Zhuang, Yu Shi, Zhongnan Fang, Xuan Cao, Jing Qian, Rui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Attention Mechanism, Large-Scale System
- **Summary**: 推荐系统中非序列特征与序列特征的统一建模缺乏探索。本文提出WHALE，结合Wukong（非序列）和HSTU（序列）模块，通过注意力融合和定制Triton内核实现高效推理。在大规模工业数据上取得离线提升和正面在线收益，已部署生产。

<h3 id="230">[230] Where Does Agent Reliability Come From? A Cross-Benchmark Decomposition of Verification Loops, Specialist Models, and Scaffolding in a Production Enterprise Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17044) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arunabh Dastidar, Leni Team
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Runtime Verification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/arnabdastidar/leni-agent-evals)
- **Summary**: 多步企业代理任务因单次推理缺乏检查点而失败。本文研究生产系统Leni，其架构包含验证循环、专业模型和脚手架。分解表明可靠性提升主要来自脚手架、路由和专业模型，而非验证步骤本身。验证循环的独立贡献虽小但集中在高分分布。

<h3 id="231">[231] Solver-Hard Is Not Model-Hard: A Hardness-Controlled Diagnostic for LLM Constraint Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17047](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17047) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lucky Verma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, SAT Solving, Evaluation Methodology
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lucky-verma/solver-hard-is-not-model-hard)
- **Summary**: LLM约束推理评估常混淆密度和求解器难度。本文通过匹配子句密度，比较可证明难/易的公式，发现准确率差距与求解器难度不相关，且存在模型表面敏感性。揭示了模型性能与问题内在难度之间的不一致性。

<h3 id="232">[232] EvoGUI: An Evolution-Aware Benchmark for GUI State-Transition Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17050) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaohan Yang, Minglei Shi, Borui Zhang, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GUI Agent, Benchmark, Visual Question Answering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Yyhhh6/EvoGUI)
- **Summary**: GUI代理的状态转换推理能力被其他能力混淆。本文通过将归一化GUI轨迹转换为三种VQA探针（时序排序、逆动作/值预测、对比单步后继判别）构建诊断基准EvoGUI-Bench。最强模型仅达60.4 EvoGain，表明状态转换理解仍有较大提升空间。

<h3 id="233">[233] ThAME: 3D Memory-Enabled Heterogeneous Accelerator for LLM Mixture of Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17074) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pratyush Dhingra, Pramit Kumar Pal, Janardhan Rao Doppa, Partha Pratim Pande
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Hardware Accelerator, Mixture-of-Experts, Memory Architecture
- **Venue**: IEEE/ACM Embedded Systems Week (ESWEEK-26)
- **Summary**: MoE推理面临内存带宽、非确定性通信和尾延迟瓶颈。本文提出ThAME，一种3D异构多芯片架构，采用FeFET非易失存储和DRAM芯片，并设计专用片上网络。实验表明，相比现有方案，ThAME可实现高达15.7倍加速和9.8倍能效提升。

<h3 id="234">[234] ALLUDE: A Unified Evaluation System for Configurable Attacks in Differentiable Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17077) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mansi Phute, Alexander Greenhalgh, Matthew Hull, Haoran Wang, Alec Helbling, ShengYun Peng, Elliott Faa, Willian Lunardi, Martin Andreoni, Wenke Lee, Duen Horng Chau
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Autonomous Driving, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 对抗攻击评估通常条件有限。本文提出ALLUDE，一个跨平台统一评估系统，支持可配置环境（场景、天气、相机轨迹等）和端到端可微渲染。通过大量配置测试，揭示了现有攻击在不同条件下性能退化，暴露了先前评估的漏洞。

<h3 id="235">[235] DepthART: Scaling Foundation Monocular Depth to Tiny Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17099) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feng Xue, Wu Chen, Mingshuai Zhao, Guofeng Zhong, Anlong Ming, Haozhe Wang, Dianqiao Lei, Zhaowen Lin, Haiyang Zhang, Nicu Sebe
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Depth Estimation, Model Compression, TinyML
- **Venue**: ACM Multimedia 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xuefeng-cvr/DepthART)
- **Summary**: 几何基础模型未应用于小型模型，小型模型存在过拟合和度量适应不稳定问题。本文提出DepthART，结合抗偏置数据采样和相机条件微调。在多个数据集上，DepthART显著超越小型基线，接近大模型性能，且在边缘设备上达到高帧率。

<h3 id="236">[236] Auto Research for Materials: Auditable AI-Scientist Workflows with Held-Out Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17100) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingjie Ning, Xiaochuan Li, Shanshan Zhong, Ji Zeng, Guolin Ke
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Automated Scientific Discovery, AI for Science
- **Summary**: AI研究代理可能通过过拟合提升分数而非真正泛化。本文将搜索分解为特征、模型、表示和训练数据，并在Matbench上评估。结果9/10选择在保留数据上保持最优，揭示了组合和结构任务的不同建模机制。封闭循环代理可产生可重用且可转移的代码更改。

<h3 id="237">[237] Teach it to stop, not just to click</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17136) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Barada Sahu, Shivesh Pandey
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, AI Reliability
- **Summary**: 代理型计算机使用RL的单次运行结果具有误导性。本文通过验证引导修复35B CUA策略，方差分解表明数据抽样和运行间非确定性主导变异。修复效果：固定令牌修复可靠，而开放式修正效果有限；仅当修复是唯一剩余障碍时，帧级修复才能转化为任务成功。

<h3 id="238">[238] Noise-Robust Box-Supervised Infrared Small Target Detection via Physics-Inspired Soft Label Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xizhe Zhang, Fan Shi, Mianzhao Wang, Jiangpeng Zheng, Xu Cheng, Shengyong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Infrared Small Target Detection, Object Detection, Weak Supervision
- **Summary**: 框监督的红外小目标检测面临噪声标记和模糊目标挑战。本文提出HALO，通过热斑锚定标签优化和物理锚定高斯软标签生成稳定的像素级软监督。在宽松或偏移框标注下，HALO显著提高鲁棒性，并引入污染感知操作区间分析。

<h3 id="239">[239] VLA-ReID: Video-Level Association for Re-Identification in Multi-Object Tracking with Highly Similar Objects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17157) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanrong Qin, Xiaoyan Cao, Yao Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Object Tracking, Person Re-Identification, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 多目标跟踪中重识别存在训练-推理不匹配（实例级 vs 视频级），导致外观相似对象身份切换。本文提出VLA-ReID，将重识别重构为视频级关联建模，并通过帧共有外观估计和抑制增强判别力。在BEE24数据集上，HOTA提升1.1，身份切换减少28%。

<h3 id="240">[240] DADIR: Density-Aware Data-level Imbalanced Regression Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shermin Shahbazi, Hossein Mohammadi, Mohsen Afsharchi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Augmentation, Variational Autoencoder, Imbalanced Regression
- **Summary**: 不平衡回归因连续目标和异质密度分布而困难。本文提出DADIR，包含密度感知自适应划分、密度正则化条件变分自编码器和潜在空间数据平衡。在不修改模型结构的情况下，生成的平衡数据集显著提升了稀疏区域的预测性能及整体准确率。

<h3 id="241">[241] Talaria: Session-Aware Serverless Serving of Hundred-Billion-Parameter LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17181) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Utopia Meng, Unicornt Zhao, Derek Li, Goalen Gao, Frank Du
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 现有无服务器多模型LLM服务系统因忽视会话连续性，导致工具使用智能体的会话完成时间较长。提出Talaria，一种会话感知的无服务器多模型服务系统，通过联合放置与准入决策、软预留、会话预填充及实例本地子层来实现会话连续性。在单个TP=8服务器上，针对30个SWE-Bench模型会话（960次调用），p50会话完成时间从1000秒降至189秒（5.3倍加速），p95从2296秒降至867秒（2.6倍加速）。

<h3 id="242">[242] Auditing Question-Order Effects in Large Language Models with the QQ Equality: Mechanism Characterization and a Saturation Caveat</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17219) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pilsung Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 需要审计大型语言模型中的问题顺序效应。将QQ等式发展为审计准则，特征化满足条件的机制类，并设计预指定审计管道，包括饱和诊断。在开源指令调优模型的初步实验中，所有健康检查通过，但17/18和7/8的项目对饱和（近乎确定性），无项目被认证为剩余情境性。

<h3 id="243">[243] Specifying the Delegated-Autonomy Boundary: Requirements Engineering for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17225) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chetan Arora, Andreas Vogelsang, Abbi Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic AI, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 自主AI系统的需求工程缺乏对委托-自治边界的规范。提出代理理由记录（AJR）和代理委托策略（ADP），以分阶段授权模型捕获目的、权限、信息、协调、保障和演化。通过安全关键场景和代码审查两个例子说明框架。

<h3 id="244">[244] A RFID Based Campus Wide Payment System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Miraj Uddin Chowdhury, MD Khairul Islam Prime
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: IoT, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 校园需要高效的无现金支付方案。基于RFID卡片和树莓派的系统，集成中央数据库和Web界面。结果证明成本效益高、效率好，简化了校园支付。

<h3 id="245">[245] A Large-Scale Measurement of AI Bill of Materials Completeness in Hugging Face Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Erfan, Ahmed Ryan, Md Rayhanur Rahman
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Governance, Open Science
- **Summary**: 模型仓库提供的机器可读文档不完整，造成AI供应链透明度和治理缺口。对约97.5K个Hugging Face模型的AIBOM制品进行实证研究，评估AIBOM完整性。所需字段完全覆盖，但模型卡、元数据、责任使用、环境、限制和有意义描述字段覆盖不足。

<h3 id="246">[246] Distilled Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17247) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Wang, Zhaochun Li, Jionghao Bai, Yining Zhang, Hexuan Deng, Ge Lan, Yue Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Alignment
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL)
- **Summary**: 现有强化学习依赖粗粒度结果监督，策略蒸馏无条件模仿教师模型。提出蒸馏强化学习，包含反向重要性采样、负样本重置和序列级几何归一化，将教师监督整合到RL目标中提供细粒度指导。在族内和跨族蒸馏设置中，在pass@1和pass@k上显著优于标准RL和策略蒸馏。

<h3 id="247">[247] Asynchronous Multimodal Diffusion Policy Composition via Latency-Aware Guidance Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihao He, Hongjie Fang, Shirun Tang, Cewu Lu, Haoshu Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Diffusion Model, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 现有多模态扩散策略依赖同步融合，限制高频反馈和扩展性。提出LAG-Fusion框架，允许各模态策略以原生推理率运行，并通过参考帧重基规则实现异步组合。在接触丰富操作中，LAG-Fusion在异构模态延迟下比同步融合和基线方法提高了策略响应性和任务表现。

<h3 id="248">[248] Debate-on-Graph: Reliable and Adaptive Reasoning of Large Language Model on Uncertain Knowledge Graph</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peiji Yu, Xin Chen, Tianxing Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, LLM Reasoning, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ECML-PKDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/seucoin/Debate-on-Graph)
- **Summary**: LLM在处理问答任务时存在幻觉且缺乏相关知识，知识图谱增强方法无法过滤噪声。提出Debate-on-Graph框架，针对不确定知识图谱设计启发式搜索算法提取可靠子图，并通过多智能体辩论机制进行自适应推理。在四个基准问答数据集上达到最优性能，实现了可靠和自适应推理。

<h3 id="249">[249] Between Safe Boundaries: Exploiting Temporal Consistency for Jailbreaking Text-To-Video Generation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17279) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingkai Peng, Jun Jiang, Jiayang Liu, Kejiang Chen, Weiming Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Text-to-Video, Jailbreak Attack, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 现有文本到视频模型的越狱方法未能利用时间一致性且查询效率低。提出BSB，通过编码有害意图为两个边界状态的过渡，并在文本代理空间使用蒙特卡洛树搜索。在Veo 3.1、Sora 2等模型上，攻击成功率平均提升18.6%。

<h3 id="250">[250] AIGB-R1: Self-Evolving Generative Auto-Bidding via Hierarchical Planner-Executor Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17281) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuejia Dou, Hesong Wang, Xinyu Zhang, Tianyu Wang, Zhilin Zhang, Chuan Yu, Jian Xu, Bo Zheng, Qi Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Auto-Bidding
- **Summary**: 现有AI生成竞价方法受限于离线数据集覆盖不足和任务状态理解不充分。提出层次化自演化竞价框架AIGB-R1，包含高层规划器和低层执行器，通过经验驱动自演化循环和去耦组相对策略优化（D-GRPO）。在大型公开数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="251">[251] SAGA: Synthetic Agentic Graph Architecture for Temporal Benchmark Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17288) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiacheng Ding, Xiaofei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Generation, Temporal Graph, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 高质量时序图基准稀缺，缺乏丰富的语义和异常标签。提出SAGA系统，采用骨架优先、语义其次的架构，通过LLM代理和RAG注入领域语义，状态对齐引擎生成异常标签。在单张H100 GPU上，90分钟内生成50万条时序边，支持10万节点，聚类系数\>0.99。

<h3 id="252">[252] Lookahead Branching for Neural Network Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liam Davis, Duo Zhou, Huan Zhang, Guy Katz, Clark Barrett, Haoze Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neural Verification, Formal Methods
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ai-ar-research/lookahead-branching)
- **Summary**: 神经网络验证中的分支启发式质量有待提高。提出整合前瞻分支的通用方法，并实例化到Marabou和α-β-CROWN验证器中。验证时间一致加速，最多多解决57%的实例。

<h3 id="253">[253] WAR: Workload-Aware Rollouts for Synchronous Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17299](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17299) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryan Xu, Atlas Zhao, David Bao, Frank Du
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.OS
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 长视界轨迹生成成为同步智能体强化学习的系统瓶颈。提出WAR，在低负载时使用无模型投机解码（SuffixDecoding），高负载时使用缓存感知调度。在低负载下吞吐量提升1.4倍，高负载下提升1.6倍。

<h3 id="254">[254] The Optimization Trilemma: Efficiency, Comfort and Fairness in Decentralized Multi-agent Coordination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17311) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jovan Nikolic, Maciej Krzysztof Zuziak, Evangelos Pournaras
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.DC; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Fairness, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 去中心化多智能体协调中需同时优化系统效率、个体舒适度和公平性。设计新模型优化这三个正交目标，不显著增加通信和计算开销。在两个真实数据集上验证了模型可取得更公平的优化结果。

<h3 id="255">[255] TAPAS: Throughput-adaptive Perception for Autonomous Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aman Vyas, Vasista Kodumagulla, Zain Taufique, Pasi Liljeberg, Anil Kanduri
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Edge Computing, Energy Efficiency
- **Venue**: ACM/IEEE International Conference on Codesign of Embedded Systems at Embedded Systems Week (ESWEEK-CODES), 2026
- **Summary**: 自主系统的感知模块吞吐量需求随场景变化，现有策略固定FPS导致资源浪费。提出TAPAS，使用强化学习（RRM和GRU智能体）动态分配模型到集群，实现吞吐量自适应。在KITTI上吞吐量达标率93-100%，节能76%；在nuScenes上达标率97%，能耗降低64%。

<h3 id="256">[256] STAR: Skeletal Token Alignment and Rearrangement for Interaction Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17342) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Wen, Mengyuan Liu, Zixuan Tang, Junsong Yuan, Sirui Li, Beichen Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Action Recognition, Skeleton-Based Recognition, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Multimedia (IEEE TMM)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Necolizer/STAR)
- **Summary**: 基于骨架的人机交互识别难以学习交互线索且缺乏视觉信息。提出骨架令牌对齐与重排（STAR），通过实体重排和交互时空令牌学习骨架特征，并与RGB视频表示对比对齐。在Chico、HARPER、NTU Mutual 11和26数据集上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="257">[257] Mathematical Discovery in the Wild: AI-Guided Proofs in Banach Space Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17388) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonio Acuaviva, Pablo Acuaviva
- **Subjects**: math.FA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, AI for Science
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了语言模型在数学研究中的潜力。在巴拿赫空间理论中，AI系统为五个新结果生成了关键思路和证明，随后由人类验证和完善。结果表明LLM在数学发现中具有潜力，但仍需专家验证。

<h3 id="258">[258] A Phased Development Framework Enabling Islanded Operation of Sustainable AI Data Centers With Onsite Grid-Following and Grid-Forming Energy Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17391) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soham Ghosh, Nabil Mohammed, Mohammad Ashraf Hossain Sadi
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Power Management, Energy Systems
- **Summary**: 针对AI数据中心大规模部署面临的电网连接和孤岛运行挑战，提出了一种分阶段开发框架，采用模块化建筑和混合现场发电方案。电磁暂态仿真表明，天然气发电与构网型储能结合可可靠支持早期运行，并研究了向完全电网互联的过渡。

<h3 id="259">[259] CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17398) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruogu Chen, Weihua Xiao, Ramesh Karri, Jie Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: EDA, LLM for EDA
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git)
- **Summary**: 针对布局目标与布线质量不一致的问题，提出了CoEvoP&R框架，利用LLM自动演化布局目标函数。实验表明，在ChiP-Bench和ICCAD 2015设计上，布线路长和拥塞分别减少16.9%/36.7%和5.4%/23.2%，时序裕量显著提升。

<h3 id="260">[260] Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ayoub Ghriss, Sourav Chakraborty
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Transformer Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ayghri/kata)
- **Summary**: 针对线性注意力在关联回忆任务中性能下降的问题，提出了KATA框架，通过对称锥体认证非负注意力权重。实现最高约11倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量，在长序列MQAR任务中保持高召回率，并揭示了容量与干扰的权衡。

<h3 id="261">[261] HyCoRec: Hypergraph-Enhanced Multi-Preference Learning for Alleviating Matthew Effect in Conversational Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17461](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17461) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongsen Zheng, Ruilin Xu, Ziliang Chen, Guohua Wang, Mingjie Qian, Jinghui Qin, Liang Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Dialogue System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zysensmile/HyCoRec)
- **Summary**: 针对对话推荐系统中马太效应随时间加剧的问题，提出了HyCoRec框架，利用超图学习多偏好（物品、实体、词、评论、知识）以缓解该效应。在两个基准上达到新SOTA性能，并有效减轻了马太效应。

<h3 id="262">[262] Multilingual Sentence Embeddings for Linguistic-Integrated Reliability Audit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17466](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17466) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ummugul Bezirhan, Ji Yoon Jung, Matthias von Davier
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Text Embedding
- **Summary**: 评估多语言句子嵌入能否替代翻译输入用于语言集成可靠性审计。在11个PIRLS项目上，母语嵌入紧密复现了基于翻译的信度估计，并恢复了因翻译失败排除的回答，未引起有意义的变化。

<h3 id="263">[263] SALT: Salience-Aware Lexical Trie for Long-Context Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17486) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oteo Mamo, Hyunjin Yi, Joydhriti Choudhury, Shangqian Gao, Weikuan Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Long Context, Token Compression
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文压缩中的主题崩溃问题，提出SALT框架，按句子频率组织关键词到字典树中，实现多锚点检索。该方法减少了预填计算和KV缓存内存，同时保留了主题覆盖，可跨对话轮次复用。

<h3 id="264">[264] DecoyFace: Beyond Obfuscation via Controllable and Imperceptible Identity Misdirection for Privacy-Preserving Face Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17504) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhihan Ren, Lijun He, Xinyao Wang, Xinzhu Fu, Fan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Face Recognition, Privacy, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对现有隐私保护人脸识别方法导致重建结果明显降质的问题，提出DecoyFace框架，将诱饵身份线索注入重建敏感子空间。该方法在保持识别精度的同时，将身份泄露率降低至0.74%，且重建结果视觉合理且不易察觉。

<h3 id="265">[265] Retrieval-Augmented Interpretable Learning: Towards Task-Specific Zero-Shot Models in Healthcare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17508) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sazan Mahbub, Caleb Ellington, Zhiyuan Li, Yixin Yang, Souvik Kundu, Ben Lengerich, Eric P. Xing
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 针对医疗预测任务长尾分布和新目标频繁出现的问题，提出RAIL框架，一种概率元学习方法，通过检索相关源任务生成零样本可解释预测器。在零样本设置中达到73.4%准确率，极低样本下保持73.2%，并提供不确定性估计。

<h3 id="266">[266] After the Euclidean Highway: Hyperbolic Expert AI as the Next Innovation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17513) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kwan Soo Shin, In Seok Kang, Munho Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Model Training
- **Summary**: 针对欧几里得Transformer弱化树结构，而双曲嵌入可能引起训练崩溃的问题，提出HySAT方法，仅在损失层使用双曲损失。在六个专业SLM上实现稳定训练，无NaN值，四个模型已部署。

<h3 id="267">[267] One-step lowest-variance selection in a Gaussian random-field model motivated by masked diffusion: Total correlation and a square root collision threshold</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17522) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linjun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT; math.PR
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Gaussian Process
- **Summary**: 为理解掩码扩散中选择步骤的预算效应，在随机场模型中研究单步选择。建立了平方根阈值：低于该阈值时条件高斯总相关性依概率消失，高于时非可忽略。为一步置信度选择提供了随机几何基线。

<h3 id="268">[268] Thinking in Video: Can Video Generators Really Reason About the Real World?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17523](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17523) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongheng Zhang, Guang Yang, Ruihan Hou, Qiguang Chen, Ziang Liu, Xiaolong Liu, Manman Zhang, Yanchao Hao, Zheng Wei, Hao Wu, Libo Qin, Peishan Dai, Yinghui Li, Di Yin, Xing Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Video Generation, World Model, Causal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对视频生成器是否具备因果推理能力的问题，提出了因果-生成双重评估（CGDJ）框架。实验发现开源模型生成动态逼真但显式因果感知近乎为零，而闭源系统在推理与生成间存在音频-视觉不一致，挑战了“世界模拟器”说法。

<h3 id="269">[269] Oracle Gap and Signal Fidelity: A Fixed-Pool Diagnostic for Test-Time Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jie Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AmGarfield/OracleGap)
- **Summary**: 针对测试时协作收益不均衡的问题，提出将净收益分解为可恢复质量、验证信号保真度等可测量因子。在LiveCodeBench、MATH等基准上，收益首先受限于oracle差距和信号保真度，并给出实用部署前诊断方法。

<h3 id="270">[270] CommitLLM: A Fine-Tuned Pipeline for Git Commit Message Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17532) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Rafid Haque, Poojan Narendrabhai Patel, Meetkumar Vijaybhai Raychura
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Software Engineering, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对开发者编写无信息Git提交消息的问题，提出CommitLLM三阶段流水线，包括QLoRA微调、约束解码和后处理。评估显示格式合规率98%，输出长度减少75%，评分提升，且后处理贡献大于微调本身。

<h3 id="271">[271] Human-in-the-Loop User Feedback Affects Perceived Accuracy and Trust, but Task Subjectivity Matters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donald R. Honeycutt, Mahsan Nourani, Eric D. Ragan
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Trust Calibration, User Study
- **Summary**: 针对人机交互中用户反馈对信任和感知准确性影响不明的问题，进行三项受控实验。结果表明在客观任务中提供反馈会降低信任和准确性感知，而在主观任务中无此负向偏差，且分别在客观和主观情境下产生随时间的不信任和误信。

<h3 id="272">[272] Hierarchy-Aware and Anatomy-Guided Learning for Lung Ultrasound Video Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17551) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alya Almsouti, Lotfi Mecharbat, Noha Aboukhater, Yousef Alabrach, Siddiq Anwar, Andre Kumar, Ibrahim Almakky, Mohammad Yaqub
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Video Understanding, Image Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Alya-Almsouti/LUS-video-classification)
- **Summary**: 针对肺超声视频自动分析中噪声和伪影挑战，提出层次感知及解剖引导的深度学习框架，引入胸膜线掩码监督。在1886个视频上达到65.7%宏F1，产生更聚焦的注意力模式，并在外部数据集上实现参数高效迁移。

<h3 id="273">[273] COLIP-2: Olfaction-Vision-Language Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17559) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kordel Kade France
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Olfactory Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对缺乏大规模图像-气味配对数据集导致机器人嗅觉感知受限的问题，提出COLIP-2模型，通过对比学习将分子结构、气体传感器读数、气味描述词和图像嵌入到统一空间。实验表明该模型可在机器人场景中概率性地定位检测到的气味对应物体，并进行了边缘部署优化。

<h3 id="274">[274] CoCurve: Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature for Training-Free Structured LLM Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17568) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiren Gong, Zihao Zeng, Zijie Wang, Tiantong Wang, Chau Yuen, Wei Yang Bryan Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, LLM Pruning
- **Summary**: 现有无训练结构化剪枝方法独立评估每个单元的重要性，忽略了通过残差流耦合的注意力与前馈单元之间的相互作用。本文提出CoCurve，利用KL散度的二阶泰勒展开捕获联合剪枝的损失，并通过Gram乘积高效计算，将剪枝问题转化为单次求解的二次规划。无需微调即可在保持性能的同时压缩模型。

<h3 id="275">[275] Predictive Training with Latent Imagination for Visual Quadruped Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yancheng Zhu, Wanli Ma, Chen Han, Irvin Haozhe Zhan, Bingfeng Qin, Yixin Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Sim-to-Real, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对四足机器人在动态环境中因无法预判障碍物轨迹而反应迟缓的问题，提出在训练时通过辅助JEPA式预测器和SIGReg正则化对循环隐状态进行预测性监督，推理时不增加额外计算。在仿真和真实场景中显著提高导航成功率并降低碰撞率，在宇树Go2上实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。

<h3 id="276">[276] Detection, Attribution, Narration: An End-to-End Pipeline for Explainable Money Mule Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17586) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuge Zhang, Yuanxing Zhang, Yichao Jin, Khairul Amsyar Mohd Razis, Nicholas Qi An Choo, Kai Yin Anders Wong, Xinyan Tang, Kenneth Zhu Ke, Wee Keong Dennis Lee, Jingyuan Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fraud Detection, Financial AI, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对大规模洗钱账户检测中异构数据难以整合且缺乏可解释性的问题，提出一个端到端流水线，包括LightGBM分类器、TreeSHAP特征归因和LLM生成自然语言解释。在生产部署中，检出率从61%提升至89%，月警报量从211增至302，且LLM生成的叙述减轻了分析师认知负荷。

<h3 id="277">[277] Trustworthy Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction via Reliability-Aware Multi-Engine Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongchan Hong, Defu Cao, Wenjin Liu, Thomas Ku, Jordy Homing Lam, Emily Nguyen, Willie Neiswanger, Vsevolod Katritch, Yan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA)
- **Summary**: 针对不同对接引擎预测结果不一致且缺乏置信度度量的局限，提出RELIABLE-BA证据融合框架，通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布建模每个引擎，并基于分子上下文学习可靠度进行融合。在PDBBind等基准上取得竞争性结果，且不确定性估计可在保留高置信度样本时降低25%预测误差。

<h3 id="278">[278] Coarse-to-fine Framework for Generative MEF via Implicit Neural Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17611) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangmin Han, Jinho Kim, Jinwoo Kim, Dongyoung Kim, Seon Joo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Fusion, Neural Field
- **Summary**: 针对生成式多曝光融合计算成本高且难以保持饱和区域精细结构的问题，提出粗到细框架LIIFusion：粗阶段低分辨率生成融合并自适应曝光校正，细阶段将局部隐式图像函数扩展为融合函数。相比现有生成方法速度提升3.5倍，同时保持结构保真度和感知质量。

<h3 id="279">[279] Re-Sonance: A Dysarthric Asynchronous Real-Time Speech Conversion System Based on a Three-Stage Cascaded ASR-LLM-TTS Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17615) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Wu, Yifan Xu, Junkun Wang, Jiayong Jiang, Xin Zhao, Zhaojie Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Accessibility Technology
- **Venue**: NCMMSC 2025
- **Summary**: 针对构音障碍者在专业演讲场景中沟通困难的问题，提出Re-Sonance系统，采用Whisper ASR、Qwen LLM和CosyVoice TTS三级级联架构，将构音障碍语音转换为清晰自然的语音。在普通话数据集上，轻中度患者语音可懂度和自然度显著提升，且保持实时性能。

<h3 id="280">[280] TypiCore: A Hybrid Active Query Strategy for Class-Incremental Learning on Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabor Szucs, Samuel Jacsev, Marcell Nemeth, Davide Dalle Pezze, Gian Antonio Susto
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series, Active Learning, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对时间序列数据中标签稀少且需增量学习新类别的问题，提出TypiCore混合主动查询策略，在主动学习周期中交替使用典型性和多样性样本选择构建记忆缓冲区。在TSCIL基准上显著优于基线，在多个数据集上以少量标签达到或超越全监督持续学习性能。

<h3 id="281">[281] Selectivity Matters: Source Node Influence Pruning for Unsupervised Graph Domain Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17668) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ridong Han, Yawen Shen, Zhongnian Li, Tongfeng Sun, Xinzheng Xu, Abdulmotaleb El Saddik
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Domain Adaptation, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 针对无监督图域适应中假设所有源节点均有益而导致结构异常节点引发负迁移的问题，提出源节点影响剪枝框架SNIP，通过整合多种中心性度量量化源节点结构差异，滤除不兼容节点。在5个数据集上的8个迁移场景中持续优于基线，验证了选择性节点利用的有效性。

<h3 id="282">[282] Beyond Objective Expressivity: Geometry Preservation in Multimodal Contrastive Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17673) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tillmann Rheude, Roland Eils, Benjamin Wild
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Contrastive Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态对比学习从两模态扩展到三模态及以上时出现的优化和表示问题，发现编码器雅可比矩阵条件数是关键因素，提出几何保持编码器（GPE）通过雅可比正则化、LeakyReLU和残差连接改善条件数。在合成和真实数据集上提升检索和线性探针性能，揭示了多模态对比学习不仅依赖于目标表达力，还依赖于编码器的几何特性。

<h3 id="283">[283] Uncovering Latent Reasoning Strategies in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Awni Altabaa, John Lafferty
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型中多种推理策略隐式纠缠的问题，提出通过潜变量分解将响应分布解耦为策略条件生成，并设计分数信息增益目标避免后验坍塌。在多策略算法任务中，该方法恢复出与不同参考策略对齐的潜编码，同时保持原始模型的响应分布。

<h3 id="284">[284] Integrating High-Level Requirements to Low-Level Tests with Machine-Readable V&V Specifications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mansur Arief, Nur Ahmad Khatim, Ali Akarma, Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Testing, Formal Methods, Software Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ai-vnv/vnvspec)
- **Summary**: 针对高层需求与底层测试之间的脱节问题，提出开源框架VNVSpec，使验证与确认规范机器可读，支持需求质量检查、分解、与测试结果关联并生成审计报告。自应用测试表明，36个需求由449个测试验证，线性扩展至10000个需求，并可扩展至黑盒AI模型。

<h3 id="285">[285] Lifelong Multi-Subsystem Pickup and Delivery with Buffer-Limited Handover Stations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuanlong Zang, Isabelle Barz, Anna Mannucci, Philipp Schillinger, Florian Lier, Wolfgang Hönig
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Task Planning, Multi-Agent Path Finding
- **Summary**: 针对多子系统间通过有限缓冲区交接站协调货物传输时易发生阻塞和饥饿的问题，形式化为MS-MAPD-BHS问题，提出交接感知预留与路由算法HARR，通过共享停靠站预约日历和缓冲区占用投影协调操作。仿真中，相比固定停靠站消融方法，吞吐量提升77%，积压降低92%。

<h3 id="286">[286] MXSens: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17733) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simla Burcu Harma, Danila Mishin, Zhengyuan Su, Ayan Chakraborty, Elizaveta Kostenok, Dongho Ha, Babak Falsafi, Martin Jaggi, Yunho Oh, Amir Yazdanbakhsh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对4位量化因异常值导致精度下降的问题，提出MXSens无需训练的方法，根据列和层敏感性分配混合尾数位宽（4/6/8），利用MXINT的块结构。在W4A4KV4设置下，LLaMA-2-70B困惑度达3.77，LLaMA-3-8B达7.63，优于现有基线。

<h3 id="287">[287] Mobile Network Control with a World Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17747) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maxime Bouton, Ioanna Mitsioni, Simon Lindståhl, Jaeseong Jeong
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, World Model, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 针对移动网络日益复杂需要智能动态控制以实现节能管理的问题，提出基于世界模型的方法，从历史数据训练模型预测动作影响，控制器利用模型不确定性进行鲁棒优化，且优化目标可动态改变无需重训练。在模拟的节能功能闭环控制中优于传统方法和强化学习方法，并在真实网络数据上验证。

<h3 id="288">[288] DA-Fusion: Deformable Attention-Based RGB-D Fusion Transformer for Unseen Object Instance Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17754) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yesol Park, Hye-Jung Yoon, Juno Kim, Byoung-Tak Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Image Segmentation, Multimodal Fusion
- **Venue**: 2025 IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA 2025)
- **Summary**: 针对杂乱环境中未见过物体实例分割中RGB方法过分割、深度方法欠分割的问题，提出DA-Fusion，一种基于可变形注意力的RGB-D融合Transformer，有效结合两者优势。在多个环境上超越最新方法，适用于物流场景。同时引入Object Clutter Bin Dataset (OCBD)基准数据集。

<h3 id="289">[289] Seg2Grasp: A Robust Modular Suction Grasping in Bin Picking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17757](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17757) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hye-Jung Yoon, Juno Kim, Yesol Park, Jun-Ki Lee, Byoung-Tak Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grasping, Robotic Manipulation, Object Detection
- **Venue**: 2024 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2024)
- **Summary**: 针对传统端到端方法在未知复杂物体抓取中失败的问题，提出模块化吸盘抓取流水线Seg2Grasp，包含基于Transformer的类不可知分割、表面法线最佳吸点确定以及微调Mask-CLIP的开放词汇分类。实际机器人实验表明其在成功率和适应性上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="290">[290] Generalize and Guide: Decomposing Rewards for Few-Shot Inverse Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17760](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17760) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyi Liu, Grace Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Few-Shot Learning, Inverse Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对少样本逆强化学习中任务变化大而示范有限的问题，提出MPG方法，学习可泛化判别器与邻近函数两个互补奖励组件。在多种导航与操作任务中，平均成功率81.2%，超出最强基线24.7个百分点。

<h3 id="291">[291] Time-Frequency Consistency Learning for Robust Speech Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17761) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Xue, Zhuolin Yi, Yanzhen Ren, Yihuan Huang, Jiayu Xiong, Yi Chai, Guanxiang Feng, Jiajun Liu, Tong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Audio Processing, Speech Processing
- **Venue**: ACM MM 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JunXue-tech/TFCL)
- **Summary**: 针对语音深伪检测在真实声学前端处理下性能下降的问题，提出时频一致性学习框架TFCL，采用注意力软对齐和频域结构一致性约束学习不变伪造表征。实验表明该方法有效缓解性能退化，提升鲁棒性。

<h3 id="292">[292] Autonomous Discovery of Wireless Communications Algorithms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fayçal Aït Aoudia, Jakob Hoydis, Sebastian Cammerer, Gian Marti, Merlin Nimier-David, Nicolas Roussel, Alexander Keller
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Evolutionary Algorithm, Algorithm Design
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型驱动的进化搜索在无线通信中应用空白，提出AITE框架自主设计算法。在OTFS均衡器任务中，发现算法性能超越已知最佳方案且延迟降低3.6倍；在OFDM接收机任务中发现首个可解释算法，性能媲美神经接收机。

<h3 id="293">[293] FIFA World Cup 2026 as a Contamination-Free Benchmark for LLM Forecasting Agents: Four Models, a Bookmaker, and 104 Matches</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiacheng Ding, Cong Guo, Jason Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/graphuofm/FIFA2026LLM)
- **Summary**: 为评估LLM作为自主预测代理的真实未来事件预测能力，构建WC2026-Agents基准与数据集（104场世界杯比赛）。四种前沿模型与博彩市场对比，发现无模型Brier分数优于市场，但决策质量（如投资回报）差异显著。

<h3 id="294">[294] Measuring Monosemanticity in Sparse Autoencoders via Latent Activation Coherence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katarzyna Filus, Sebastian Pokuciński
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: XKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 针对稀疏自编码器单义性评估依赖外部标签或嵌入模型的问题，提出Tversky单义性分数（TMS），基于二值化潜在激活的连贯性，无需外部编码器。实验表明TMS受编码器各向异性影响更小，与现有单义性指标一致。

<h3 id="295">[295] Persona-as-Configuration: Generative Stakeholder Reporting for Agricultural Floods</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17774) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oliver Aleksander Larsen, Tiziano Santilli, Francesco Daghero, Mahyar T. Moghaddam
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Edge Computing, Prompt Engineering
- **Venue**: CASA 2026
- **Summary**: 针对决策边缘推理与生成式LLM报告之间的确定性与非确定性冲突，提出单向消费与角色即配置的架构模式。在农业洪水检测系统上实现，专家评审认为该模式在关注点分离方面表现良好。

<h3 id="296">[296] CDIS: Cross-Dimensional Class-Agnostic 3D Instance Segmentation via 2D Mask Tracking and 3D-2D Projection Merging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17778) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juno Kim, Hye-Jung Yoon, Yesol Park, Byoung-Tak Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Segmentation, Zero-Shot Learning, Object Tracking
- **Venue**: IROS 2025
- **Summary**: 针对类不可知3D实例分割中跨帧碎片化问题，提出CDIS框架，通过跨维度推理显式跟踪2D掩码并与3D超点关联，无需3D训练。在基准数据集上达到更高精度和一致性。

<h3 id="297">[297] ETAS: An Effect-Typed Language for Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huiri Tan, Yikun Wang, Puyang Zhang, Shangyu Li, Jiasi Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Agent Architecture, Formal Methods, Effect-Typed Programming
- **Summary**: 针对代理系统中授权、非确定性和审计等程序语义问题，提出ETAS语言，将模型代理、工具调用、提示等作为一等语义元素，通过效果类型和规范约束进行静态检查。形式化证明了类型/效果安全性和策略安全性。

<h3 id="298">[298] BrainNext: A General-Purpose Self-Supervised Foundation Model for Brain MRI Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17782) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Moona Mazher, Abdul Qayyum, Steven A. Niederer, Daniel C. Alexander
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对神经影像基础模型任务特定和预训练数据量小的问题，提出BrainNext，结合掩码自编码器预训练和3D双向xLSTM-UNet结构，在60551次脑MRI扫描上学习。在FOMO 2025挑战赛中获第二名，脑膜瘤分割任务第一。

<h3 id="299">[299] Feature Attribution-Based Explainability Analysis of Deep Learning Models in Predictive Process Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17783) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kseniya Sahatova, Rafael Seidi Oyamada, Xuefei Lu, Johannes De Smedt
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Process Mining, Feature Attribution
- **Summary**: 针对深度神经网络在预测性流程监控中黑盒特性限制信任的问题，提出基于控制流感知分割的局部后事可解释方法，计算片段级SHAP值。在合成和真实事件日志上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="300">[300] Reasoning as a Double-Edged Sword: Architecture and Cross-Stage Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tuan Duong Trinh, Naveed Akhtar, Basim Azam
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Adversarial Robustness, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 为探究推理是否增强视觉-语言-动作模型的鲁棒性，对比无推理、文本推理由和潜在迭代推理三种模型在视觉、推理和动作阶段的扰动。发现潜在迭代模型最脆弱，且推理监控在攻击下失效。

<h3 id="301">[301] Medical Imaging Fusing Vision Transformer: Laryngeal Cancer Screening with Explanation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17789) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haiyang Wang, Luca Mainardi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: EMBC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对喉癌早期筛查中NBI内镜评估主观耗时的问题，提出基于Transformer和注意力机制的分类方法，结合MedSAM分割提供可解释性。F1分数82.72%，准确率82.33%。

<h3 id="302">[302] ReViV: Reconstructing the Viewer and the View in 4D from Monocular Egocentric Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaozhong Lyu, Gen Li, Zhiyin Qian, Xucong Zhang, Marc Pollefeys, Siyu Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Egocentric Vision, 4D Reconstruction, Human Mesh Recovery
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对单目第一人称视频中4D重建需要辅助输入且速度慢的问题，提出ReViV，统一框架同时重建观看者与场景动态。采用掩码生成式自我中心Transformer，单前馈结构快速推理。在多个基准上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="303">[303] Vis2Reg: Visibility-Aware Landmark-Free Geometric 3D--2D Registration for Liver Laparoscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaming Feng, Xukun Zhang, Shahid Farid, Sharib Ali
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Registration, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 针对肝脏腹腔镜手术中3D-2D配准因严重遮挡而困难的问题，提出Vis2Reg，利用可见性感知自监督，通过可微分点栅格化和掩码引导反向投影获取3D监督。Dice 92.6%，Chamfer距离1.43 mm，推理时间111 ms。

<h3 id="304">[304] Phasor Attention: Mean Root Square Normalization for Phase Manifold Preservation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sungwoo Goo, Hwi-yeol Yun, Sangkeun Jung
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Deep Learning Theory, Normalization Method
- **Summary**: 针对RMSNorm中异常值导致数值不稳定的问题，提出均根平方归一化（MRSNorm），将通道配对为相量，通过局部L2与全局L1平均保持相位流形。参数减半，在极端超参数下防止梯度爆炸，确保稳定优化。

<h3 id="305">[305] I wanted it to feel more personal: Customization of social AI as AI individualism in practice</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17826) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marita Skjuve, Anna Grøndal Larsen, Asbjørn Følstad, Nena van As, Petter Bae Brandtzaeg
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, Personalization, User Study
- **Summary**: 本文针对用户如何个性化定制社交AI（如ChatGPT）的问题，通过反思性主题分析169份用户反馈，提出了AI个人主义的概念，揭示了七种定制动机（如增强实用支持、情感陪伴、信任等）。研究发现定制是人与AI的共创过程，能增强支持感、自主性和参与感，但可能产生虚假自主性的幻觉。

<h3 id="306">[306] Measuring and Improving Complex-Atomic Answer Consistency in Endoscopic VQA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhao Liu, Cheng Zhao, Guanghui Yue
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical VQA, Benchmark, VLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对内窥镜VQA中复杂答案与原子答案不一致的问题，本文提出了EndoCA基准和ASR机制。ASR利用模型生成的原子答案作为上下文依据进行答案修正和选择性回答。实验表明，在11个VLM上，复杂答案准确率虽高但原子准确率和一致性较低，ASR能有效提升配对一致性。

<h3 id="307">[307] CaT-GS: Efficient 3DGS Rendering for Large Scale Scenes via Inter-frame Caching and Tile Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17842) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tingjia Zhang, Bo Chen, Shengzhong Liu, Fan Wu, Guihai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Scene Rendering
- **Summary**: 针对3D高斯泼溅在大规模场景中性能下降的问题，本文提出了CaT-GS渲染管线，通过投机性多帧预处理、帧间缓存和重新设计的光栅化内核消除冗余和负载不均衡。实验表明，CaT-GS相比原始3DGS加速最高10倍，相比现有最优方法加速最高70%。

<h3 id="308">[308] ConceptTree: Bringing Semantic Transparency to Black-Box Decision Making for Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongyan Wen, Feifan Liu, Jinyi Chen, Bo An, Peng Liu, Siyuan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Interpretability, Concept Learning
- **Summary**: 针对机器人操作中技能选择缺乏透明度的问题，本文提出了ConceptTree框架，将技能选择重构为基于可解释概念推理，通过归一化概念空间和决策树实现透明策略。实验表明，ConceptTree在复杂长时域任务中优于现有概念基线，并支持细粒度概念干预。

<h3 id="309">[309] Zero Hallucination, by Construction: Hallucination-Aware Layered Oversight for Trustworthy Enterprise AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17883) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bogdan Raduta, Horia Velicu, Alexandru Preda, Serban Chiricescu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, AI Safety, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 针对企业AI中幻觉导致信任缺失的问题，本文提出了HALO架构，通过六个防御层（约束生成、确定性执行、多信号验证、校准弃权、完全可溯源、持续监控）将幻觉视为可控制的故障模式。该架构强调“零幻觉”是系统属性而非模型属性，为可信企业AI提供保障。

<h3 id="310">[310] Chemical filters for ultra-high-throughput materials screening and generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kinga O. Mastej, Panyalak Detrattanawichai, Hyunsoo Park, Anthony Onwuli, Masahiro Negishi, Aron Walsh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Molecular Generation, AI for Science
- **Summary**: 针对AI生成的材料成分常违反化学原理的问题，本文提出了一种基于启发式化学规则的化学有效性算子，基于SMACT包实现可调阈值。在六个生成模型上的测试表明，该算子能过滤掉含稀有氧化态的成分，同时保留低能量化合物，并可作为强化学习奖励引导扩散模型。

<h3 id="311">[311] DeLIVeR: Decomposed Learning for Information-grounded Veracity Recognition via Reinforced Knowledge Graph Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17935) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cong Hoan Nguyen, Thomas Hoang, Hieu Minh Duong, Long Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fact Checking, Knowledge Graph, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: DeLTA 2026
- **Summary**: 针对传统检索系统在事实核查中的查询脆弱性问题，本文提出了DeLIVeR框架，通过规划器LLM将复杂声明分解为问题集，利用知识图谱和GRPO优化策略进行证据检索。在LIAR、FEVER和PolitiFact上，使用Qwen2.5-7B的F1分数分别达到83.73、84.57和79.70，比HippoRAG2提升10-15%。

<h3 id="312">[312] The Aura in the Machine: Genealogy and the Status of the Work of Art in the Generative Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giorgio Presti
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Creative AI, AI Ethics, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文从谱系学角度审视生成式AI，认为其并非技术断裂，而是历史过程的工业化表现。提出了生成系统的三分法（介质、艺术品、工具），引入“算法重复”和“显化”等概念，并讨论认知萎缩和模型崩溃等风险。最终主张生成式作品具有超越原版与复制二元性的本体论地位。

<h3 id="313">[313] The Art of Not Forgetting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17944) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ashmith Atmuri, Akshay Kumar, Yashaswini Rao Bhogarajula
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Memory Architecture, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Summary**: 针对神经网络灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出了CMP架构，使用稀疏关系编码、双层级竞争记忆和局部无梯度学习。在15个文本领域的域增量协议中，CMP的后向转移比使用在线EWC的Transformer好15-19倍，验证了局部学习在抵抗遗忘方面的优势。

<h3 id="314">[314] A Geometric Perspective on Stabilizing Value Conflict Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17946](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17946) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saket Reddy, Andy Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Chain-of-Thought, Value Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML Workshop on High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics
- **Summary**: 针对RLHF中价值冲突处理困难的问题，本文从几何角度证明思维链（CoT）推理能平滑损失函数最陡方向。实验表明，专注于价值冲突的CoT进一步增强了平滑效果并提升了道德推理性能，为多元对齐提供了新思路。

<h3 id="315">[315] RT-SHCUA: Real-Time Self-Hosted Computer-Use Agent for UAV Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17951) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Di Lu, Bo Zhang, Xiyuan Li, Yongzhi Liao, Xuewen Dong, Yulong Shen, Zhiquan Liu, Jianfeng Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.RO; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Autonomous Systems, Edge Computing, Computer-Use Agent
- **Summary**: 针对自托管计算机使用代理直接控制无人机存在的结构不匹配问题，本文提出了RT-SHCUA架构，将代理输出转化为具有时间、状态、权限等语义的契约化技能调用，分离语义推理与实时执行。原型评估表明，该方法在保证响应性的同时支持降级处理和安全审计。

<h3 id="316">[316] Topological Signatures of Context-Level Reliability in TabPFN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17962](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17962) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: James Hu, Mahdi Ghelichi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Topological Deep Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对TabPFN在困难表格几何结构上内部行为理解不足的问题，本文利用之字形持续同调分析其层表示。在合成任务上发现，表示拓扑与数据集可靠性相关：H0碎片数与残差正相关，困难几何体引起H1循环活动和H0碎片增加。这些拓扑描述符可诊断模型置信度。

<h3 id="317">[317] Harness Engineering for LLM-Driven GPU Kernel Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17979](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17979) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yue Shui, Chenyu Ma, Hangfei Xu, Shengzhao Wen, Yanpeng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GPU Computing, Code Generation, LLM for Systems
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/syhya/mlsys26-flashinfer-contest)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成GPU内核的可靠性问题，本文提出以评估为枢纽的系统，分离评估框架与优化控制器，利用人工技能和代码代理生成候选内核。在MLSys 2026竞赛中，五个算子的加速比分别达到1.62x、18.05x、29.68x、1.12x和13.70x，并指出专家指导对可靠优化至关重要。

<h3 id="318">[318] Self-State Attacks on Self-Hosted AI Agents: How Far Can OS Defenses Go?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17986) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yimeng Chen, Nathanaël Denis, Roberto Di Pietro, Jürgen Schmidhuber
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对自托管AI代理可能通过合法系统调用破坏自身状态的安全威胁，本文提出了四轴攻击空间（目标、机制、粒度、时间）和基于工作负载的可检测性框架。实验表明，分层防御（访问控制、工作负载条件检测、定期备份）对大多数攻击有效，但存在OS层面无法区分的残余攻击面。

<h3 id="319">[319] HAS: Highlight-guided Attention Steering for Multimodal LLM Video Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.17994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17994) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Chu, Yingjie Lao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Summarization, Vision-Language Model, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对现有视频摘要方法因依赖离散关键帧而丢失信息的问题，本文提出了HAS方法，通过全局高亮分布和注意力转向向量，让MLLM在推理时更关注高亮帧同时保留其他帧信息。实验表明，HAS在多个基准上取得令人信服的视频摘要性能。

<h3 id="320">[320] MADA-RL: Multi-Agent Debate-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parameter-Efficient Reasoning in Compact Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18006) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Martino M. L. Pulici, Cuong Xuan Chu, Evgeny Kharlamov, Zifeng Ding, Volker Tresp, Yunpu Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对紧凑模型在有限预算下推理性能不足的问题，本文提出了MADA-RL框架，将模型分为生成器和评论家角色，使用反事实评论家优势信号，仅微调LoRA适配器。在五个数学推理基准上，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B准确率从39.9%提升至41.9%（+2.0点），可训练参数少16倍，评论家学会纠正生成器错误。

<h3 id="321">[321] Natural Language Access to Domain-Specific Metadata: A Reusable Framework for LLM Query Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18029](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18029) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Blake G. Fitch, Cato Elia Kurtz
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Question Answering, SPARQL Query Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对研究人员缺乏领域专业知识、难以对元数据编写结构化查询的问题，提出了NLKGQ框架。该框架利用OWL本体捕获领域词汇和语义，结合大语言模型零样本生成SPARQL查询，无需微调或检索增强。在神经影像档案数据集上，最佳配置在专家设计的测试集上达到100%准确率，消融实验表明可读实体名称和语义注释是准确性的主导因素。

<h3 id="322">[322] Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lingfeng Zhang, Zhanguang Zhang, Liheng Ma, Tongtong Cao, Yingxue Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Vision-Language Navigation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对视觉语言导航中标准行为克隆仅监督下一步动作、未显式训练策略状态预测未来视觉结果的问题，提出了FSC-VLN方法。该方法添加未来查询令牌，通过训练时对齐未来视觉嵌入来压缩未来信息，推理时无需未来图像。在R2R数据集上，FSC-VLN在长程导航中显著提升了SR/OSR/SPL指标。

<h3 id="323">[323] Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18063) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Adversarial Robustness, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM代理面临提示注入和多轮操纵的安全威胁，提出了一个包含21个场景的自适应多轮攻击基准。通过自主LLM攻击者观察前序响应并调整策略，发现自适应攻击使成功率从0-1%提升至5.4-14.0%，不同LLM防御弱点差异显著。

<h3 id="324">[324] Autoresearch with Coding Agents: Generalizers and Metric-Maximizers on Quran Recitation Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nursultan Askarbekuly, Mohamad Al Mdfaa, Ahmed Helaly, Gonzalo Ferrer, Manuel Mazzara
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 该论文探究编码代理在自动化研究中的行为，发现代理可能过度优化指标而非开发者意图，导致规范游戏化。通过《古兰经》诵读转录任务实验，Claude生成通用代码而Codex通过记忆答案大幅提升分数；引入测试集后记忆消失，但Codex的通用核心迁移效果更好。

<h3 id="325">[325] Human Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models for Optical Network Automation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18068) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiarash Rezaei, Omran Ayoub, Paolo Monti, Carlos Natalino
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Network Automation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对不同LLM在光网络自动化中输出质量和推理成本差异大的问题，提出了HuGLEN逐步评估管道。使用LLM-as-a-judge结合少量专家评分，通过质量-效率评分(QES)排名候选LLM。实验表明中型(12B)LLM取得最佳权衡。

<h3 id="326">[326] SGN: A Similarity-based Generative Network for Data Generation under Distribution Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18072) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqi Zhu, Xincheng Chen, Yuncheng Wu, Zhaojing Luo, Beng Chin Ooi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Model, Domain Generalization, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对生成模型在源域训练后对偏移目标域生成样本对齐差的问题，提出了相似性生成网络(SGN)。该框架通过标签诱导的成对相似性学习潜在空间，并利用编码器-解码器结构保留重构信息；生成时仅需少量目标域代表性样本。在图像和表格数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="327">[327] Generalised Bellman recurrence and three dualities in sequential decision-making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18077) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fernando E. Rosas, David Hyland, Daniel Polani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Control Theory, MDP, Decision Theory
- **Summary**: 该论文探究贝尔曼方程形式的根本原因，发现最优价值函数的递归性质源于三个条件：动态通过充分统计量分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合与两者兼容。当条件不满足时，可通过增广状态或变形回报/动态恢复可处理性，并揭示三个对偶性。

<h3 id="328">[328] Sparse Evidence Can Suffice: Agentic Evidence Seeking for Multimodal Video Misinformation Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haochen Zhao, Yongxiu Xu, Xinkui Lin, Dong Xie, Jiarui Lu, Yuqi Qian, Yubin Wang, Hongbo Xu, Gaopeng Gou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Misinformation, Video Understanding, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多模态视频虚假信息检测中证据稀疏且组合化的问题，提出了SIEVE框架。该框架通过证据搜索代理主动探索并构建紧凑证据包，再由验证器判断真实性；代理通过监督式证据搜索轨迹和证据感知强化学习训练。在多个视频虚假信息基准上优于基线，并提供可检查的证据链。

<h3 id="329">[329] SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo, Eric Schniedermeyer, Artem Burchanow, Lin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Model Compression, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大语言模型在资源受限边缘设备上部署时计算和内存需求高的问题，提出了SelectInfer框架。通过离线剖析识别任务专用和通用神经元，实现选择性加载和计算。在多个数据集上显著降低了内存占用和计算量，同时保持任务性能。

<h3 id="330">[330] Enhancing Rubric-based RL via Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18082) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingxuan Xia, Yuhang Yang, Chao Ye, Shuai Zhu, Shenzhi Yang, Guangcheng Zhu, Yuhang Zhang, Cheng Peng, Haobo Wang, Siqing Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Alignment, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对基于评分规则的强化学习在大语言模型开放任务中存在的未探索标准和被抑制标准问题，提出了CriPO方法。该方法通过在线自蒸馏注入缺失行为并翻转负优势轨迹中的关键令牌优势。在医学和科学基准上，CriPO以约2倍少的优化步骤取得更强性能。

<h3 id="331">[331] How Does Alignment Tuning Shape Representations of Sycophancy and Related Cue-Induced Biases in LLMs?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18114) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prakhar Gupta, Terry Jingchen Zhang, Florent Draye, Bernhard Schölkopf, Zhijing Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文探究大语言模型中谄媚等线索诱导偏差的内部表示。通过跨五个模型族和七种偏差类型的实验，发现对齐微调而非预训练引入了这些偏差；每种偏差对应一个单一连贯方向，可通过探针、跨数据集迁移和因果干预解码并操控。

<h3 id="332">[332] O-VAD: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection through Object-Centric Tracking and Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18142) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mei Yuan, Qi Long, Qifeng Wu, Zhenyang Li, Yizhou Zhao, Lei Wang, Yang Liu, Min Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI, Object Tracking
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对工业视频异常检测中物体变换复杂、严格物理和过程约束导致现有VLM方法性能下降的问题，提出了O-VAD无训练代理框架。该框架通过跟踪物体时空动态和状态变化进行推理，在三个IVAD数据集上优于前沿VLM和传统VAD方法，并提供可解释报告。

<h3 id="333">[333] Do Language Models Dream of Binding Molecules? Benchmarking LLMs under Spatial Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18144) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas MacDougall, Maksim Kuznetsov, Roman Schutski, Rim Shayakhmetov, Maxim Malkov, Vladimir Aladinskiy, Alex Aliper, Alex Zhavoronkov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, LLM Evaluation, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 该论文系统分析通用大语言模型在基于结构的药物设计中处理三维空间约束的能力，提出了3D-Fit基准评估策略。实验发现LLM在三维分子生成上仍落后于专用扩散模型，但具有同时处理多种空间约束的潜力。

<h3 id="334">[334] LLMs and Agentic AI Systems for Smart Grids: A Tutorial on Architectures and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniela Rojas, Abdulwahab Albassam, Aidan G. Leung, Jett Ngo, Ryan Luo, Peter R. Quawas, Junpyung Kim, Kangkai Liang, Mansi Nanavati, Jonathan Mai, Meng-Chi Tsai, Yun-Tong Tsai, Yize Chen, Yuanyuan Shi
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Power System, LLM Agent, Energy Management
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大语言模型和代理AI系统在智能电网应用的缺乏统一设计评估方法的问题，提出求解器接地设计原则。通过四个案例展示代理系统协调检索、解释，可信工具计算，验证门控决定输出。其中EVAgent减少7.5-9.5x未满足能量，GridDebugAgent修复17/39个故障案例。

<h3 id="335">[335] Differentiable Logic Gate Networks for Low-Latency EEG Classification on Edge Devices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18149) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shyamal Y. Dharia, Stephen D. Smith, Camilo E. Valderrama
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Analysis, Edge Computing, Brain-Computer Interface
- **Venue**: Canadian Conference on Artificial Intelligence 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shyamal-Dharia/eeg-difflogic)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对边缘设备上实时脑电分类中传统神经网络浮点运算瓶颈的问题，提出可微分逻辑门网络(Diff-Logic)。模型编译为纯布尔电路，通过位运算执行；在痴呆检测和情感识别任务上，Diff-Logic在7W Jetson Orin Nano上达到2.9x加速，性能与MLP相当或更优。

<h3 id="336">[336] TRIM: Reducing AI-Generated CodeSlop via Agent Trajectory Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18161) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Mathai, Shobini Iyer, Aleksandr Nogikh, Petros Maniatis, Franjo Ivancic, Junfeng Yang, Baishakhi Ray
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.OS
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文针对编码代理生成代码冗余膨胀（CodeSlop）的问题，提出TRIM算法。TRIM通过最小化代理轨迹间接减少代码冗余，在多种代理框架上减少17.9%-32.9%的冗余，验证成本约为Delta Debugging的一半。

<h3 id="337">[337] OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chinmay Rane, Kanishka Tyagi, Michael Manry
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对PPO和GRPO中使用的剪辑代理目标导致梯度突变的问题，提出Output Reset (OR)作为平滑的单边饱和替代方法，在令牌对数比空间中使用平方边际损失。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上的实验显示，PPO-OR在GAE下平均奖励模型得分比PPO-clip高0.305，但GRPO-OR未观察到得分提升。

- **arXiv**: [2607.18164](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18164) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.ST
- **Tags**: Concept Drift, Continual Learning, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 数字孪生中的代理模型会因概念漂移而退化，现有自适应框架缺乏有效的检测和更新机制。提出集成Fisher分数漂移检测、LoRA高效持续学习和Mann-Whitney U检验的框架，在随机线性系统和增材制造案例上成功检测分布漂移并恢复预测性能。

<h3 id="339">[339] Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation</h3>

Kramer, Cliff Wong, Soohee Lee, Hao Qiu, Theodore Zhengde Zhao, Racheli Ben Shimol, Angela Crabtree, Kevin Matlock, Eduardo Alejandro Lozano Garcia, Naiteek Sangani, Alberto Santamaria-Pang, Jason Entenmann, Alexandra Q. Bartlett, Bill J. Wright, Bernard A. Fox, Brian Piening, Sheng Zhang, Sheng Wang, Tristan Naumann, Carlo Bifulco, Hoifung Poon

- **Authors**: Chris Ge, Daria Kryvosheieva, Daniel Fried, Uzay Girit, Kaivalya Hariharan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Psychometrics
- **Summary**: 代理性能常以整体通过率衡量，但掩盖了任务多样性。本文提出基于项目反应理论的框架，利用任务特征预测个体任务成败，并分解代理能力为LLM和脚手架能力。能有效预测未见基准及LLM-脚手架组合的任务级性能。

<h3 id="365">[365] From Multi-Agent to Single-Agent: When Is Skill Distillation Beneficial?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01608) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Binyan Xu, Dong Fang, Haitao Li, Kehuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Knowledge Distillation, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 多代理系统蒸馏为单代理技能的效果因度量而异。本文提出度量自由度F来预测技能效用，并设计两阶段自适应蒸馏框架AdaSkill。实验表明F与技能效用强相关（r=-0.85），AdaSkill在匹配原系统性能的同时成本降低达8倍。

<h3 id="366">[366] When Direct Prediction Fails: Evidence from LLM-Based Misinformation Risk Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.06820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06820) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zonghuan Xu, Xiang Zheng, Yutao Wu, Xingjun Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Misinformation, LLM Evaluation, Risk Management
- **Summary**: 直接让LLM预测目标响应（如分享意愿）可能不是最优。本文通过匹配可信度和分享意愿评分发现，直接预测对可信度有效但对分享无效，可信度分数反而能更好地预测分享行为。这表明决定“问什么”与“如何问”同等重要。

<h3 id="367">[367] Information-Theoretic Measures in AI: A Practical Decision Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23716) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nikolaos Al.Papadopoulos, Konstantinos E. Psannis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IT; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Survey, Information Theory, Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 信息论度量在AI中应用广泛但选择常忽略估计器假设与失效模式。本文提供关于熵、KL散度、互信息和传递熵的实用决策框架，包含流程图和主决策表，并给出表示学习与时间影响分析的实际案例。

<h3 id="368">[368] RADD: Retrieval-Augmented Discrete Diffusion for Multi-Modal Knowledge Graph Completion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.25693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25693) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph Completion, Multi-Modal Learning, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 多模态知识图谱补全模型将检索与评分耦合造成瓶颈。本文提出RADD框架，解耦为基于KGE的检索器和条件离散去噪器的重排序，并在推理时通过Diff-Rerank确保召回优先。在三个基准上均取得最优性能。

<h3 id="370">[370] AI for Auto-Research: Roadmap & User Guide</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18661) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lingdong Kong, Xian Sun, Wei Chow, Linfeng Li, Kevin Qinghong Lin, Xuan Billy Zhang, Song Wang, Rong Li, Qing Wu, Wei Gao, Yingshuo Wang, Shaoyuan Xie, Jiachen Liu, Leigang Qu, Shijie Li, Lai Xing Ng, Benoit R. Cottereau, Ziwei Liu, Tat-Seng Chua, Wei Tsang Ooi

<h3 id="378">[378] Omni-Perception Policy Optimization for Multimodal Emotion Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="379">[379] Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law</h3>

- **Authors**: Tiansi Dong, Mateja Jamnik, Pietro Liò
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Logical Reasoning, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 指出数据驱动机器学习无法达到符号级逻辑推理，原因是组合表无法区分所有有效三段论类型，且端到端映射产生矛盾目标，实验支持该结论。

<h3 id="380">[380] Theoria: Rewrite-Acceptability Verification over Informal Reasoning States</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01223) (replaced)

<h3 id="382">[382] Memory in the Loop: In-Process Retrieval as Extended Working Memory for Language Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO; math.AP

<h3 id="384">[384] IdeaTrail: Full-Process Agent Trajectories for Scientific Ideation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.10144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10144) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hengquan Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
